**Code Architecture**


**Key Technical Concepts**
- **Next.js Static Export ():** This is a core constraint. It prevents the use of dynamic API routes in the production build, forcing a static data-fetching strategy (e.g., generating a JSON file at build time).
- **GSAP & ScrollTrigger:** Used for homepage animations. The agent learned to handle animations in nested components and refined timings.
- **Client-Side Storage Management:** Fixed a  quota error by separating large binary data (images) from structured cart data, storing images in IndexedDB and only lightweight cart details in .
- **File System Operations (Node.js):** Used  module in API routes (in dev) and build scripts to read directories and write files, essential for the dynamic mask selector and saving product data.

**Critical Info for New Agent**
- **Work from  branch:** The agent is currently on the  branch fetched from the user's repo. All new work should stem from this branch. Start by analyzing its contents.
- **Static Export is Law:** The project uses . Do not implement dynamic server-side API routes for the frontend. They will work in  mode but **will fail the production build**. Any dynamic data must be handled via build-time generation (static JSON files) or client-side fetching to external services.
- **JSON is the Single Source of Truth:** All product data logic has been consolidated to use . Do not re-introduce the  file for product data.
- **Communicate First, Code Second:** The user has expressed significant frustration with the agent making unrequested changes or implementing overly complex solutions. Confirm your plan and understanding of the requirements **before** writing code, especially when modifying existing functionality.
